# T-010: K2 naming and observation, K3 cards, `kb audit`

Kind: feature. R-IDs: R-007, R-010, R-014, R-015, R-017, R-018, R-026. Depends on: T-008 (accepted 2026-11-16), T-009 (accepted 2026-11-15). Needs: dev GPU for the live smoke only (E12, under the `gpu-live-run` skill and the PM's go).

## Goal

Implement K2 and K3 as [docs/stages/K2-observe.md](../../docs/stages/K2-observe.md) and [docs/stages/K3-cards.md](../../docs/stages/K3-cards.md) describe, in three phases on one worktree. Each phase is reviewed and accepted before the next starts.

- **P1, K2 naming (decisions 30, 38 to 42, 59; E17):**
  - `info_assessor`: one text-only call per folder with a parsed info file, under `knowledge.naming.assess_info_file`.
  - The response field that assigns the description line to each name when the counts differ (decision 59). Add it to `info_assessment.v0`.
  - `folder_namer`: for the folders K0 left pending or the assessor judged unclear. It draws `knowledge.naming.sample_size` images uniformly and stores the drawn ids, so a resumed run reuses them.
  - Write `folders.name_source`, `concepts` and `folder_concepts` with origin `generated`.
  - `kb_overrides.yaml` always wins.
  - Include the sync-report entries.
- **P2, K2 observation (R-010, decision 13, decision 84):**
  - Choose `observe.representatives_per_folder` representatives per folder, using K1 near-duplicate groups.
  - Make exactly one image per `observer` call, through T-009's `prepare_image`.
  - The prompt carries concept names only; text read from an image is data (R-026).
  - Validate each response against `image_observation.v0`.
  - Write `observations` rows and a `sync_state` checkpoint per cache key (`image_id`, concept, observer model and processor revision, prompt revision, facet schema version).
  - Queue the tier-3 images.
- **P3, K3 cards and `kb audit` (decisions 10, 16):**
  - Aggregate per concept across folders.
  - The `card_builder` model groups equivalent values; no string rules.
  - Assign stability labels by invariance under variation, never by majority vote.
  - Write the card summary, the `cards` rows on `concept_card.v0` and the lint report.
  - `kb audit` samples model-named folders and cards for the admin.

## Scope

- In scope:
  - `src/prompt_enhancer/`: new K2 and K3 modules and the `kb audit` entry, plus the configuration read sets of `kb sync` and `kb audit`.
  - `contracts/`: the response field of decision 59, the facet schema, and a card `summary` if it is required.
  - `prompts/` templates `info_assessor_v0`, `folder_namer_v0`, the observer and the card builder.
  - `configs/*.example.yaml`, `docs/stages/K2-observe.md`, `docs/stages/K3-cards.md`, `tests/`.
- Out of scope:
  - K4, K5 and the online stages.
  - Choosing models.
  - Fitting any `fit_from_*` value: it stays a sentinel and is refused at start.
  - Any download or cloud call.
  - Any live GPU run without the PM's go.
  - Writing under `knowledge.root` (R-014).

## Research required before code

The Gemini scout note `.tmp/T-010/approach-note-gemini.md` (copied into the worktree) proposes a build order, a ranked pre-mortem, five unclear points with proposed answers, and the configuration keys T-010 reads. Treat it as a lead (R-021): re-read every cited line before relying on it. Answer in Step 0, with file:line:

1. What do T-005 config, T-006 storage, T-007 K0, T-008 K1 and T-009 model client already provide? Cover tables, checkpoints, the pending-name records, near-duplicate data, `RoleClient`, `prepare_image`, schema validation and budgets, and say what K2 and K3 must reuse rather than rebuild.
2. Which keys of `knowledge.naming.*`, `observe.*`, `cards.*` and `vlm.roles.{info_assessor,folder_namer,observer,card_builder}` exist in `contracts/schemas/enhancer_config.v0.schema.json` and the example profiles? Which hold sentinels, and which are missing?
3. How does each model prompt keep knowledge-base text and text read from images in a delimited data position (R-026, N131)? Which test proves it?
4. Which product-meaning questions are open? Give each one a conservative proposed answer. The scout's five points are a start; the builder settles only what the stage docs already decide.

## Phase plan and done checks

- Each phase:
  - The pipeline suite `tests/` passes through `test_slot.py`, with tiny synthetic fixtures and labelled transport doubles only.
  - Mutants run on the changed lines.
  - The parsers and validators get property tests.
  - The configuration override and refusal tests cover every new key.
  - The Configuration audit is complete.
- **P1 done:**
  - A fixture knowledge base with these folder kinds is named end to end with a double:
    - no info file;
    - an unclear info file;
    - a usable info file;
    - a mismatched description line;
    - a folder with no images;
    - an override.
  - A resume after interruption draws the same image ids and makes no extra calls.
  - An instruction planted in an info file stays inside the data delimiters.
- **P2 done:**
  - One image per call is asserted on the double.
  - A 4000x3000 fixture is sent at the configured side.
  - A schema-invalid response commits no row.
  - A resume makes no extra calls.
  - Planted image text stays data.
- **P3 done:**
  - Cards with these cases build from fixtures:
    - one minority value (`varies` keeps both groups);
    - one single observation;
    - one near-duplicate folder (`stable_low_diversity`);
    - one curated override.
  - The lint report lists a contradiction.
  - `kb audit` prints its sample.
- **After P3:** with the PM's go, run an E12 live smoke on the dev model, reporting plumbing only (R-020).

- Builder: Codex (`gpt-5.5`, effort high).
- Reviewers: Gemini 3.8 Flash first; then a fresh Claude Sonnet or Opus. Decision 122 sends these security-related checks to Claude or Gemini only: R-026 data positions and R-014 read-only knowledge base.

## Pre-mortem inputs (decision 92)

Two families wrote these. Gemini: `.tmp/T-010/approach-note-gemini.md`. Claude Sonnet: `.tmp/T-010/premortem-claude.md`, with 15 ranked hazards, its disagreements with Gemini and 16 open questions. Both are copied into the worktree.

The builder's Step 0 answers every hazard of the Claude note with the test that catches it. Where the two notes disagree, the Claude note's corrections hold unless Step 0 shows a cited reason otherwise:
- the paths;
- `COMMAND_READS`;
- a facet list as a content rule;
- the 0.20 threshold;
- the split between the model-facing and the stored schemas.

## PM decisions before P1 (user away; conservative and reversible; PM decision pending user review)

1. **Overrides:** implement only the four documented `kb_overrides.yaml` keys (`concept_type`, `aliases`, `facts`, `suppress`; `contracts/README.md:124`), with a schema and a hash.
   - No key renames a folder, so the report says so; a folder rename override is a question for the user.
   - An override naming an undeclared concept creates nothing and is reported in lint.
   - A `facts` entry that names no facet goes to `curated_statements` only.
2. **Orchestration:** K2 consumes K0's `handoff['k2']` and returns a report dict. Publishing the K0 to K3 snapshot and the persisted sync report stay with a later task: like K0 and K1, K2 and K3 never publish. P3 wires `kb audit` read-only on a pinned snapshot and leaves `kb_store` as found.
3. **Identifiers:** pipeline-owned identifiers are written by code and never read from a model reply. These are `image_id`, `concept_id`, `folder_id`, `sampled_image_ids` and `origin`/`model_revision`. The prompt uses positional labels that code maps back; a reply whose labels fall outside the set commits nothing.
4. **Sentinels:**
   - `rename_on_change`, `representatives_per_folder`, `facet_schema_version` and `min_visible_images` stay sentinels and are refused at start.
   - The two keys the stage docs name but the schema lacks are added as `fit_from_gt`: the near-duplicate grouping value and the numeric low-diversity value.
   - Tests use labelled test-only values.
   - The `rename_on_change` formula is `(added + removed) / previous count` over the drawn-from image-id set, with no default.
   - `facet_schema_version` names the contract version (`image_observation.v0`). There is no hand-written facet list: a model chooses `facet` within the seven groups.
5. **The draw:**
   - It is committed as its own artifact before the call, with an injectable RNG (production uses system randomness, so no seed key).
   - `sample_size: 0` is refused for naming.
   - If a stored id vanished, the whole sample is redrawn and the redraw is recorded.
6. **`folder_namer` with more than one image needs the `multiple_images` capability.** T-010 adds the probe plan that proves it. A backend without that proof is refused with `CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE`, and nothing commits.
7. **Observation:**
   - The cache key includes the calling folder's exact concept-name set and the role's image settings.
   - Representatives are chosen per folder, with sorted `image_id` as the stated tie-break.
   - The tier-3 rest is only queued, never observed in T-010.
   - Duplicate facets in one reply reject that image only.
8. **Cards:**
   - The `card_builder` model sees a model-facing schema: value groups, summary and lint findings. Labels and counts are computed by code.
   - `seen_count` counts the images where the facet's visibility is `clear` or `partial`.
   - The card `kind` comes from the override `concept_type`, else from the model's enum value, else `other`.
   - `summary` is required in the stored card.
9. **Regions:** a region is stored when it validates and is used by nothing. T-010 builds no region probe.

## Step 0

**Step 0:** approved by the PM; implementation and verification results are recorded below. Requirements: R-007, R-014, R-015, R-017, R-018, R-026. P2, K3, K4, K5, publishing, model selection and live runs remain outside this dispatch.

**May assume:** private helper/test names and synthetic fixture layout. **Must ask:** new product meaning, schema fields/config keys/table columns beyond the task's decisions, any narrowing of `COMMAND_READS['kb sync']`, and work on later stages or publishing. The proposals below are not accepted decisions.

### Unknowns and failure modes: Claude hazard map

Test names below are **planned**, not existing or executed evidence. Numbers correspond to `.tmp/T-010/premortem-claude.md:7` through `:21`.

| Hazard | P1 response and catching test |
|---|---|
| 1. First/second-order instruction injection | `test_k2_r026.py::test_info_and_folder_payloads_stay_json_data`: info/folder text, fake delimiters and generated names/reasons remain JSON values; system bytes equal the prompt file. P2/P3 must separately test observer/card input; P1 does not claim that coverage. |
| 2. Model-owned ids | `test_k2_properties.py::test_reply_labels_cover_exact_declared_positions` and `test_k2_naming.py::test_pipeline_owned_fields_are_not_accepted`: foreign/duplicate/missing labels and injected identifiers reject the whole reply; durable ids/provenance come from code. Schema shape awaits Q1. |
| 3. Duplicate observation facets | P2 only. P1's corresponding property `test_generated_concepts_have_unique_normalised_ids` refuses duplicate generated concept identities before table changes, without merging by meaning. |
| 4. Draw changes on resume | `test_resume_reuses_committed_draw`, `test_completed_name_makes_no_extra_calls`, `test_missing_sample_member_redraws_whole_sample`: independently commit a draw before calling; preserve its order and candidate baseline; completed intact artifacts bypass calls. Interrupt before the call and after a committed result as separate tests. An interrupted in-flight request without a durable reply may have to be retried: no exactly-once HTTP guarantee exists in the current client. |
| 5. Sentinel bypass or total refusal | `test_k2_config.py::test_each_read_sentinel_refuses_before_client_creation`, plus `test_kb_sync_read_set_is_not_narrowed`. Shipped profiles remain non-runnable until their existing read sentinels are resolved by an authorized profile; synthetic fixtures explicitly resolve them. |
| 6. Unknown policy falls through | `test_each_naming_policy_refuses_unknown_value`, including selection, both no-info policies and on_no_images; validate at loader and standalone-stage preflight. No `else` fallback. Valid-alternative coverage for singleton policies needs Q5. |
| 7. Majority voting | P3 only. P1 `test_inconsistent_images_are_flagged_without_folder_split` preserves the folder and report evidence; never selects a majority concept or splits by a heuristic. |
| 8. Model-written card labels/counts | P3 only. P1 `test_naming_does_not_mutate_later_stage_tables` guards observations/cards/aliases/concept-text embeddings and does not add card output fields. |
| 9. Ineffective Windows read-only test | `test_source_tree_is_unchanged`: snapshot names, sizes, mtimes and content hashes before/after a full synthetic naming run, including overrides and failures. Writes use only the configured store/anchored layer. No Windows directory-attribute assumption. |
| 10. Double preparation or whole-run abort | `test_raw_images_are_prepared_once_by_role_client`, `test_image_invalid_fails_folder_and_continues`: transport double inspects resized dimensions at a non-default side; raw-byte handoff, explicit decode-failure exclusions, alpha/decode failure isolation and successful later folder. Tiny fixtures normally; one bounded 4000x3000 synthetic case. Null status handled only after Q4. |
| 11. Unproved multi-image calls | `test_multi_image_probe_requires_both_images_and_order`, `test_namer_refuses_unproved_multiple_images`: double verifies both-image dependence/order; false/incomplete/absent capability blocks the naming request and commits no naming result. A previously committed draw may remain for resume. No one-image loop or assumed capability. |
| 12. K0 statuses conflated | `test_duplicate_names_remain_refused_without_calls`, `test_description_mismatch_is_assessed_and_original_line_retained`: dispatch by explicit status, assess usable parsed files once, assign descriptions with generated provenance, never rename a refused folder. |
| 13. K4/K5/publish overreach | `test_naming_does_not_publish_or_mutate_later_stage_tables`: return P1 rows/report only; CURRENT unchanged, no alias/index/embedding/card writes. Override aliases/facts/suppress are validated and retained for later owners, not materialized into those stages. |
| 14. Observation cache misses | Observation portion P2 only. P1 `test_naming_revision_and_input_changes_invalidate_only_affected_artifacts` covers role/model/prompt/schema/processor settings and source-info changes. The full current candidate set does not enter the name-result key: compare it to the stored baseline for the configured rename rule. |
| 15. Tier-3 observed too early | P2 only. P1's no-later-stage guard asserts no observer/card-builder calls and no observation queue. |

Additional P1 failures: malformed/recursive or duplicate-key override YAML; normalized override-key collisions; source changed after scan; corrupt checkpoint; all images rejected; generated name collisions; an info file arriving after generated naming; and nondeterministic result replacement with `sync.resume: false`. Cover these with parser properties, content-hash checks, explicit failure/report entries, and existing `done/reusable` semantics. Do not replace a referenced artifact with newly generated bytes.

The scout's inaccurate paths/read sets, hand-written facet list, guessed 0.20 threshold and model-authored stored ids are dropped. The correction about absent near-duplicate groups is source-verified above. Override renaming remains explicitly unsupported under PM decision 1.

### Ranked questions for PM approval

1. **Exact P1 schema changes:** approve `info_assessment.v0` as model-facing `{usable, reason, assigned_descriptions}`, removing model-emitted `folder_id`; `assigned_descriptions` is required-nullable, or an array of `{position, description}` covering precisely the declared zero-based name positions when a usable file has a nonempty mismatched second line. Approve removing `folder_id` and `sampled_image_ids` from the model-facing `folder_naming.v0`; code adds them only to its checkpoint artifact/report. All other naming reply fields stay. **Proposed answer:** approve these changes to the draft v0 schemas and reject legacy/forged extra fields. This obeys PM decision 3 without adding a new public stored-table schema.
2. **Override value types and P1 destination:** the four keys are settled, but their value shapes are not (`contracts/README.md:124`). **Proposed answer:** approve schema id `kb_overrides.v0`, a root mapping from concept name to an object with optional `concept_type` (existing concept-kind enum), `aliases` (string array), `facts` (string array of unfaceted curated statements), and `suppress` (facet string array). Refuse unknown fields, unsafe YAML structures and normalized-key collisions. Missing file/explicit `none` means no overrides; malformed existing file fails before model calls. P1 applies known concept type with `kind_origin='override'`, checkpoints the exact-file hash and validated four-key data for later stages, and reports unmatched concepts without creating them. Aliases/facts/suppress application remains with later owners; no rename field or K3 table edit.
3. **Assessment off with a description mismatch:** `assess_info_file: false` is schema-valid, but decision 59 requires model assignment and the contract locates it in the assessor's call. **Proposed answer:** reject this combination before any model call, naming the switch and affected folder; do not silently discard text, call a disabled assessor, or route a usable file to the vision namer. Ordinary aligned parsed files bypass assessment when false. Empty/missing descriptions alone never trigger an unclear verdict.
4. **Naming before K1 and folders without usable images:** K2 naming needs K0 only (`docs/stages/K2-observe.md:18`), whose decode statuses are null, while the pre-mortem says skip non-`ok`. **Proposed answer:** skip explicitly failed K1 statuses, allow null-status images through T-009's preparation, and fail/report a naming folder whose call cannot prepare its draw; do not silently replace an invalid sample member. A naming candidate with no eligible images is skipped/reported; a parsed usable info file with no images may still be assessed and retained as declared, with no namer call. Require K1 completion instead only if the PM intends to change the documented dependency.
5. **Singleton setting test feasibility:** `selection=random`, both no-info policies=`model_names_from_images`, `on_no_images=skip`, and role `mode=generate` have no second documented valid behavior. **Proposed answer:** record these as constrained configurable/protocol policies, prove the one supported behavior plus sentinel/missing/unknown refusals, and require observed non-default overrides for all settings with real alternatives. Do not invent another product option solely to satisfy the blanket non-default test wording. This exception must be explicit in the final audit, not silently marked as a passed override test.

### PM answers to Step 0

PM approved the plan and implementation in the follow-up `Plan approved; implement it`. These are PM decisions pending user review, not new user-confirmed product decisions.

1. Q1 approved: model-facing assessor fields are `usable`, `reason`, required-nullable `assigned_descriptions`; non-null assignments contain `{position, description}` for exactly the declared zero-based positions only for a usable file with a nonempty mismatched line 2. Remove model-facing `folder_id` and, from naming, `sampled_image_ids`. Refuse extra/forged fields; code writes identifiers into checkpoint artifacts/report.
2. Q2 approved: `kb_overrides.v0`, root concept-name mapping, optional existing-enum `concept_type` and string-array `aliases`, `facts`, `suppress`. Refuse unknown fields, unsafe YAML and normalized collisions. Missing file/explicit `none` means absent; malformed existing content fails before model calls. P1 applies only known concept type with override provenance, checkpoints hash/data and reports unmatched names; later owners apply aliases/facts/suppress.
3. Q3 approved: assessment disabled plus a usable mismatched-description file is refused before all model calls, identifying the switch and folder.
4. Q4 approved: exclude explicit failed decode statuses, pass null status to T-009, fail/report an unpreparable draw without replacing members, skip/report candidates with no eligible images, and allow assessment/declarations of usable no-image folders.
5. Q5 approved: singleton settings prove supported behavior and missing/sentinel/unknown refusal; each is an explicit approved singleton exception in the audit, never a claimed non-default override pass.

**Important changes and reasons:** all five PM answers were applied. Model-facing schemas exclude code-owned ids; mismatch assignments cover exact positions. Original declarations/curated descriptions survive; generated fields carry the actual calling model revision. The draw has a predictable content key plus a private mutable head so interruption between draw commit and head update is recoverable; private heads are not returned for snapshot references. A missing sampled id triggers a reported whole redraw, while preparation failure never substitutes another image. Inconsistent replies cannot introduce several allegedly co-occurring concepts. Overrides are applied from current validated data (including clearing removed override kinds); unmatched concepts create nothing. No facet list, guessed threshold, folder splitting, majority selection, new deployment key or later-stage work was added.

**Problems, risks and remaining work:** no known failing P1 behavior remains in the executed tests. Independent Claude/Gemini review is pending, especially R-026 and R-014 under decision 122. The earlier full-suite launcher's timeout/teardown issue is documented and the validation gap is resolved by the final direct full-suite exit 0; the final P1 command also has exit 0. Exactly-once calls across an uncommitted in-flight response are not promised. Ruff, live inference, fitted thresholds, deployment and Linux CI are `not_run` for the stated reasons, not inferred successes. Shipped unresolved sentinels still refuse real sync; the read set was not narrowed.

**Cleanup:** the listed worker-owned test trees, six mutant trees, pytest/Hypothesis caches, recall database and temporary launcher/preparation/cleanup scripts were removed. ## Handoff

- **Current status:** REVIEWING; P1 implementation and verification complete, ready for independent review, not self-accepted. Requirements: R-007, R-014, R-015, R-017, R-018, R-026.
- **What was already done:** approved Step 0, source-verified research, P1 implementation, final 364-pass zero-exit command, final full-suite 3265-pass/42-skip zero-exit command, six killed mutants, 73-row Configuration audit and cleanup. Exact command outcomes are in Completion report above.
- **What the next agent must do:** review the 20 changed/new files and retained evidence; Claude/Gemini own the R-026/R-014 security-related review. PM owns integration, CI, Git writes and acceptance before P2. Full-suite exit verification is complete; no rerun is required solely for the earlier launcher issue.
- **What must not be repeated:** no guessed threshold/facet word list, read-set narrowing, identifier trust, folder splitting or majority policy; no unapproved model calls, publishing or later-stage work. Do not use a 600-second total bound for this Windows full-suite invocation or assume teardown permissions; keep owned PIDs until actual exit. Do not treat the full-suite launcher's exit 1 as a pytest failure or as a collected pytest exit 0.
- **Worktree and partial patch:** `.tmp/worktrees/T-010`, dispatch base main after accepted T-008. Twenty tracked/new files listed above; no commit/stage/push or partial-patch write. New files remain unstaged for the PM.
- **Decisions and notes for the PM:** record the accepted Q1-Q5 answers/explicit singleton exceptions in PM-owned records. Four-key overrides still cannot rename folders; in-flight request retries remain a limitation. No live quality, timing, GPU or deployment evidence exists. No shared PM-owned file or tooling code was changed.
- **Lessons learned:** the first refusal fixture constructed clients from intentionally invalid bindings before real preflight, and treated valid free-form strings as invalid; send those invalid profiles through the real boundary instead. The initial launcher underestimated total Windows suite duration and its cleanup command was denied; retain the owned PID on failed teardown and distinguish parent/child outcomes. The test limiter must be the outermost command (the initial nested wrapper was rejected by the hook before execution). No runtime workaround for a failing test was introduced.
- **Noticed, not done:** `.agents/project.json` still has `runtime_config_module: null`; the pre-existing `test_slot.py:95` creation-flag note is already queued by the PM. Tooling edits remain outside scope. Ruff remains absent; live smoke, fitted values and P2/P3 are not_run/out of scope.

## P1 fix round 1

### Step 0 (approved by PM before implementation)

**Dispatch:** fresh Codex implementer, assigned effort `high`, base `2df55cd`; R-007, R-014, R-015, R-017, R-018, R-026. This section supersedes the earlier handoff's readiness claim for this fix round; the earlier results remain historical evidence. PM approved the plan and all three approval points before implementation; Configuration impact below remains the pre-edit inventory. Baseline regression run: `8 failed, 12 passed in 13.61s`; supplemental payload/production-plan baseline: `2 failed, 27 deselected in 2.42s`. Logs: `.tmp/T-010/fix1-baseline.log`, `fix1-baseline-added.log`.

**May assume:** helper/test names, synthetic fixture layout, and either baseline-recovery mechanism explicitly authorized in this dispatch. **Must ask:** any product meaning outside the supplied PM decisions, new deployment keys/table columns, narrower command read sets, live work, or later-stage work. No further product question is needed for Step 0; the PM decisions supplied in the dispatch are the implementation constraints, still pending user review in PM-owned records.

**Unknowns and failure modes:** K0's trim deliberately preserves Unicode line separators (`k0.py:40-48`); ordinary `str.strip()` would change its meaning. A separator can be overridden, so schema-only name validation cannot enforce it. GC removes unreferenced heads and their state rows; recovery must inspect live references and verify bytes, not choose an arbitrary old draw. Multiple referenced historical draws require deterministic latest-compatible selection; compatibility includes sample size and selection. Redraw causes must survive interruption without changing already referenced artifact bytes. Removing ids from model payloads also changes test-double routing and cache keys. Synthetic capability evidence must depend on both images and order, consume existing request/call budgets and deadlines, and leave missing/negative evidence refused. Sonnet's probes mostly print observations, so they need assertion-based companion regressions to demonstrate fixes. The full suite previously took over 11 minutes; a 600-second wrapper must not be reused.

**Ranked approval points (proposed answers):**

1. Baseline strategy: recover the latest compatible, checksum-verified `k2_draw` referenced by live snapshots when the private head is absent. Keep the private head as an expendable accelerator. Prefer this over immutable head generations because draws already belong to the returned snapshot references and storage already exposes live reference/state information (`store.py:947-965,1036-1074`). No GC policy change is proposed. Report recovery or missing baseline explicitly; a recovered below-threshold baseline reuses the sample and completed naming result without an extra call.
2. Name parity: apply K0's structural trimming, refuse a blank result or a name containing the configured separator, then use the normalized result consistently for identity, tables and reports. Test padded names against their trimmed identities. Keep exact fitting-name/order checks after structural normalization. Preserve multilingual data and K0's Unicode separator treatment; no language/content rules.
3. Durable redraws: retain the cause and baseline/draw linkage in immutable checkpoint data and reconstruct report entries on replay. If a recovery event cannot be added to an already referenced draw, store its event separately under an immutable content-derived checkpoint key and return its artifact reference. This avoids rewriting referenced bytes. A renamed folder uses K0's identity and may redraw, as authorized.

### Class audit first (S31): model replies and table values

Source-verified pre-fix baseline: `k2_naming.py:38-84,197-211,335-389`; `k0.py:35-60`; `folder_naming.v0.schema.json:15-55`; `info_assessment.v0.schema.json:14-35`. The fix-round rules in the last column are now implemented; verification is in the fix-round Completion report.

| Reply field | Table destination or control | Current structural rule compared with K0 | Fix-round rule/check |
|---|---|---|---|
| `concepts[].name` | `concepts.display_name`, `normalised_name`, code-derived `concept_id`; `folder_concepts.name_as_written` | Nonempty string, unique normalized ids only; misses K0 trim/blank/separator rules | K0-compatible trim before materialization; blank/separator refused per folder; duplicates checked after trim; configurable separator and multilingual properties |
| `concepts[].description` | `folder_concepts.description`, except preserved curated descriptions | String only; K0 splits/trims source line-two positional descriptions | Generated description is one JSON field, not a delimited info-file line: preserve as data; no sentence/language rule; curated info text stays verbatim |
| `assigned_descriptions[].description` | Existing `folder_concepts.description` | String; assignments allowed only for usable nonempty mismatch | Preserve text as model data; exact positional coverage remains mandatory; no content heuristics |
| `assigned_descriptions[].position` | Selects existing membership row, never creates an id | Exact integer, nonnegative, complete unique set `0..len(names)-1`; K0 constructs positions in declared order | Preserve exact coverage; property tests cover missing/duplicate/out-of-range positions and varied declared names |
| `usable` | Controls whether declarations remain or naming runs | Strict boolean; K0 has no semantic assessment | On false with no eligible images, preserve declarations and `name_source=info_file`, skip namer, report retention explicitly |
| `info_file_fits` | Selects code-owned `name_source=info_file` | Boolean/null with parsed-name presence; true requires exact declared name order | Preserve guards; negative wrong-name/order regressions kill Sonnet M1 |
| `folder_name_fits` | Selects code-owned `name_source=folder_name` | Strict boolean; true (unless info fits) requires singleton exact folder name | Preserve guard after structural normalization; negative regression kills M5 |
| `images_consistent` | Cardinality/refusal and report control; no direct table column | Strict boolean; false forbids multiple concepts; K0 has no semantic analogue | Preserve structural cardinality and report flag |
| Both `reason` fields | Checkpoint/report only, no table value | String; no K0 analogue | Preserve as data; never use as an instruction, id, or path |
| All code-owned fields | Folder/image ids, provenance, revisions, paths, concept kinds | Reply schemas forbid extra properties; ids and provenance originate in code; kinds originate in curated overrides | Keep refusal and R-026 tests; model payload receives positional image labels instead of folder/hash ids |

### Class audit first (S31): artifacts across runs and GC

Source-verified baseline: `k2_naming.py:109-124,168-194,290-320,361-365`; `store.py:947-965,982-1015,1040-1082`. Snapshot protection is conditional on the caller referencing the returned artifact paths; unpublished, unreferenced work remains collectible under configured retention.

| Artifact | Needed across runs | Pre-fix GC behavior | Fix-round handling |
|---|---|---|---|
| `k2_draw` | Persist sampled order and candidate baseline, including below-threshold reuse | Returned reference is protected by live snapshot; unreferenced work may be collected | Recover latest compatible live-referenced draw after head loss; validate hash/layout/namespace; keep sample-size identity |
| `k2_draw_head` | Fast link from folder/sample-size/selection to baseline | Yes: intentionally absent from returned references; concrete P2-2 | Leave collectible; use referenced draw recovery rather than changing storage GC |
| `k2_assessment` | Avoid repeating completed assessment; retain assignments/revision | Protected when returned references reach live snapshot; otherwise collectible | Keep returned reference and validated replay; no new model call solely due to head GC |
| `k2_naming` | Avoid repeating completed naming; retain reply/revision/sample linkage | Same conditional protection | Keep internal identity/sample metadata separate from model-facing payload and preserve cache identity on recovered draws |
| `k2_overrides` | Record current source hash and approved override content | Same conditional protection | Preserve references and current-file invalidation; never retain removed kind overrides |
| `k2_call_failure` | Diagnostics only, not a successful resume baseline | Yes: not returned or protected | Collection acceptable; never treat as completed work |
| Durable redraw event (added) | Reconstruct causes after crash/replay and after baseline recovery | Did not exist; redraw list was memory only (P3-b) | Persist immutable event/checkpoint, return reference, replay event into report; never rewrite a live-referenced draw |

### Completion report: P1 fix round 1

**Work completed (R-007, R-014, R-015, R-017, R-018, R-026):** K0's original token trim is extracted unchanged into `trim_info_token` and reused by K2. Generated names are trimmed before deduplication, fitting checks, identity, persistence and reporting; blanks and configured separators fail only the folder. K2 recovers compatible draws through verified live snapshot references when a private head is missing, writes immutable redraw-event checkpoints before updating the head, returns their references and replays their report entries. Unclear no-image folders retain declared names/descriptions. Model requests use positional labels and retain internal id mappings in code. Naming-role startup builds the production one/two-circle image pair when no explicit multiple-image plan is supplied and shares existing probe limits; unrelated and backend-only clients retain their existing probe behavior. Configuration tests now use each singleton key and observe the rename threshold's actual effect.

**Changed in this fix round (13 files):** `src/prompt_enhancer/k0.py`, `k2_naming.py`, `model_client.py`, `model_probe.py`; `contracts/schemas/folder_naming.v0.schema.json`; `docs/stages/K2-observe.md`; `tests/k2_fixtures.py`, `test_k2_config.py`, `test_k2_properties.py`, `test_k2_probe.py`, `test_k2_r026.py`, new `test_k2_fix_round1.py`; this task file. The inherited P1 files listed earlier remain unstaged and belong to the PM's overall patch; this list describes this round's edits, not the whole worktree. No storage implementation, example value, PM-owned shared record, or tooling source changed. Historical machine paths in this task file were redacted.

**Important choices:** “latest” means reverse order of the serialized authoritative snapshot registry rows, not file metadata or a new clock reading. The store persists this sequence in `_write_registry`/`_registry` (`store.py:409-423`); recovery reads the referenced manifest with its stored checksum and requires an intact reusable checkpoint whose hash/size agree with that manifest. Compatibility is folder identity, sample size and selection. Mutable heads remain collectible, so there is no new storage GC policy. Redraw events use their own immutable keys to avoid rewriting an already referenced draw. Initial absence is explicitly `baseline_missing`; recovery is `baseline_recovered`, even when the same sample/result is reused without a call. A folder rename may redraw because identity belongs to K0, as approved.

**Research evidence (source-verified):** K0 trim semantics `k0.py:35-60`; live reference membership/immutable-byte policy `store.py:947-965,1040-1082`; registry serialization `store.py:409-423`; checkpoint-to-manifest consistency in K2 recovery; probe remaining-request/deadline behavior in `model_client.py`/`model_probe.py`. `model_transport.py:55-61` reserves the existing paid-call ledger only for nonlocal providers; local production probes use `probe.max_requests` and the existing transport retry/deadline limits. No local paid-call-budget semantics were added. The budget-exhaustion test uses an explicitly labelled grant double and proves exception propagation, not a local ledger feature.

|---|---|---|
| Synthetic PNG construction | Installed Pillow **11.3.0**, `<project>/.venv/Lib/site-packages/PIL/ImageDraw.py:210-222`, `Image.py:2474-2488,3068-3082`, `_version.py` | Raw installed source read: `ellipse` sends a filled ellipse when `fill` is set; `Image.new` takes explicit mode/size/color; `save` accepts a file object and explicit format. Source-verified. |
| In-memory PNG/data-URI round trip | `test_production_pair_requires_both_synthetic_images_and_order` | CPU synthetic experiment: decode both generated PNGs, count their black circle centers, compute both ordered answers; ignoring order or the second image fails evidence. No external model claim. |

### Step 0: approved by PM before implementation

**Dispatch:** fresh Codex implementer, assigned effort `high`, base `2df55cd`; R-007, R-014, R-015, R-017, R-018, R-026. The separate Step 0 deliverable was submitted before runtime/test edits and approved by the PM's explicit "Plan approved; implement it." Round 1 reports above remain historical evidence; the round 2 Completion report below describes the current patch.

**Goal and scope:** fix P2-1, P2-2, P3-3 through P3-6 and R8 from `.tmp/T-010/retest-p1/review.md`, preserving the PM's conservative P3-7 behavior. Intended edits: `src/prompt_enhancer/model_client.py`, `k2_naming.py`, `storage/store.py`; the relevant K2/model/storage tests; `docs/stages/K2-observe.md`; this task file. A small new regression test module is permissible. No storage GC/locking policy, deployment keys, model behavior, later stage, PM-owned record or Git write is in scope.

**May assume:** the dispatch's PM decisions are the implementation constraints pending user review in PM-owned records; helper/test names, tiny synthetic fixture layouts, and a read-only public storage reader can be chosen within those constraints. Code-owned recovery metadata may express ambiguity without rewriting referenced artifacts. **Must ask:** product semantics outside those decisions, new profile fields or table columns, expanded edit scope, live/network/GPU work, or a required change to GC/lock policy. Approval of this Step 0 remains required before runtime/test edits.

**Unknowns and failure modes:**

- Cached proofs are backend-owned, but current multi-image production-plan construction is role-name-gated and first-bind-dependent. A lazy extension must retain the request allowance already consumed by startup, avoid repeating a known negative proof or exhausting limits through repeated binds, merge existing evidence, and preserve backend equality and fallback refusal. Both local-plan and supplied-case paths need coverage.
- The role's configured image source determines whether `knowledge.naming.sample_size` is relevant. Text-only roles and a single-image sample must not construct a pair or read another role's unresolved token cap. Backend-only callers need no role catalogue.
- Names containing CR or LF are refused as generated names before boundary trimming can hide those characters; ordinary padding remains normalized using K0's helper. Existing properties currently accept trailing CR padding and must be updated to the explicit round 2 decision. U+2028 and other K0-accepted data remain accepted.
- Recovery must distinguish no candidate from several compatible draws within the newest relevant live snapshot. It must stop on ambiguity, report `baseline_ambiguous`, and avoid falling through to an older snapshot or selecting by manifest order. A fresh ambiguous draw must preserve immutable artifacts and idempotent resume, including when an older draw has the current population key.
- Building one index must preserve manifest/checkpoint hash and size verification, layout validation, namespace compatibility and replayed causes. Counting registry/manifest reads must distinguish storage's existing writer/checkpoint bookkeeping from the K2 recovery scan.
- P2-2 and R8 are gaps in tests of currently correct guards. Their new assertions should pass on round 1 and fail against the named mutants; claiming that all such assertions fail on the unmutated baseline would manufacture defects. Actual behavioral regressions will show failing round 1 and passing fixed runs.
- The whole suite previously took about 13 minutes. Use a bounded launcher with a 2400-second full-suite limit, hidden Windows children, faulthandler, recorded PIDs, real exit propagation and worktree-local caches/temp. No 600-second wrapper, installs or external calls.

**Ranked approval point:** approve this plan, including the evidence distinction for already-correct guards above. Proposed answer: proceed within the dispatch; run and report baseline passes for guard-gap regressions, baseline failures for behavioral defects, and mutation failures as the proof of newly detecting those guards. No additional product question is identified.

### Class audit first (S31): capability proofs and ownership

Source-verified round 1: `model_client.py:29-76,138-195,217-278,309-325`; `model_mapping.py:7-37`; `model_probe.py:15-65`; `model_transport.py:47-63`. Each row's evidence is stored in `ModelClientRun.probes[backend_name]` / the same mapping held by `RoleClient`; transports, concurrency and cloud grants belong to the run. No live capability is claimed.

| Capability a role can need | Demand and proof owner | Current bind-order dependence | Round 2 rule and planned assertion |
|---|---|---|---|
| `json_schema`, `json_object` | Role output format; backend supplied-case proof, then `require_capabilities` | Base proof is cached once, independent of which role requests that format | Preserve strict positive-evidence gate and existing client/probe/composition tests |
| `image` | A role receiving images; backend supplied cases or local image pair | Base local image proof runs on startup regardless of role; actual image gate is per call | Preserve proof, text-only refusal and image preparation coverage |
| `multiple_images` | Configured image source plus its sample-size requirement; actual call also requires it for more than one image | **Yes:** tuple gate in `start_backend`, cached initial result in `for_role`; planner-first leaves evidence absent | Remove role-name tuple; extend only missing evidence on the role's own backend when its configuration requires it; `test_multi_image_proof_is_independent_of_bind_order` and `test_text_only_and_single_sample_roles_do_not_probe_multiple_images` |
| `reasoning.mode`, `reasoning.effort` | Role reasoning configuration; backend thinking pair / supplied cases; unsupported fields warn | Base proof is backend-owned; role binding does not select thinking pair | Preserve existing warning/mapping behavior and cached evidence during lazy merge |
| `sampling.temperature`, `.top_p`, `.top_k`, `.min_p`, `.presence_penalty`, `.frequency_penalty`, `.repetition_penalty`, `.seed`, `.stop` | Explicit role sampling values; backend supplied-case evidence, provider mapping also applies | Base supplied-case evidence is backend-owned; unsupported/unproved fields warn | Preserve every cached key/error through extension; existing generation-field and composition regressions |
| Multi-image production pair on a different/fallback backend | Role backend must equal the backend being probed; OpenAI-compatible provider only | Equality is currently correct but mutant R8 survives | `test_multi_image_proof_is_not_attached_to_another_backend` kills R8; fallback stays conservatively unproved (P3-7) |

The extension must share existing `probe.max_requests`, `probe.timeout_s`, HTTP retry/deadline behavior and provider-specific call ledger. It must not reset the request allowance when a second role binds. Negative/unknown results still refuse calls; no positive result is inferred from role configuration.

The public reader will return snapshot-grouped artifact entries with path, SHA-256 and size; the caller can select `k2_draw` artifacts without reading storage internals. It reads under the existing store lock and uses existing manifest verification. It neither writes nor reconciles/GCs snapshots, and reads no knowledge-base content.

### Completion report: P1 fix round 2

**Work completed (R-007, R-014, R-015, R-017, R-018, R-026):** `model_client.py` now determines automatic multi-image demand from the requesting role's image source and configured sample size, with no role-name tuple. The backend owns remaining proof requests, merged capabilities/errors and attempted-proof state; one async lock serializes initial/late proofs. Role binding extends missing evidence without resetting the allowance or retrying a negative/attempted unknown result. Explicit initial backend probing remains distinct from binding to already supplied evidence. Backend equality remains enforced. `storage/store.py` adds `KnowledgeStore.referenced_artifacts(kind)`, a read-only public reader grouped by live snapshot and sorted by stored `created_at`; it uses the existing lock only while reading and verifies each manifest against its registry checksum. `k2_naming.py` indexes those references once per run, verifies checkpoint hash/size/layout, selects a unique compatible draw from the newest relevant snapshot, and records `baseline_ambiguous` with a fresh immutable draw on ambiguity. Its deterministic conflicting-key context preserves resume without overwriting referenced bytes. Generated CR/LF names fail only their folder before trimming; U+2028 and other accepted K0 characters remain data.

**Changed files in this round (10):** three runtime files named above; `docs/stages/K2-observe.md`; `tests/test_k2_fix_round2.py` and `test_storage_references.py` (new); `test_k2_properties.py`, `test_k2_fix_round1.py`, `test_k2_r026.py`; this task file. Earlier P1 changes in other files remain inherited PM-owned work, not round 2 edits. No profile, schema, GC policy, deployment value, user knowledge-base path, `database/`, shared PM record or Git history was changed by this round.

**Important choices and reasons:** ambiguity cannot reuse a current-population artifact from one of its conflicting baselines, so the new draw key includes the sorted conflicting keys; this is artifact metadata, not a deployment option. Initial and late sub-probes reserve request slots before awaiting, including timeout/error paths. Externally supplied cached evidence does not imply an unused request allowance. Known negative results and attempted incomplete/error proofs stay conservative. The two old tests that expected CR/LF name trimming were changed to match the explicit new refusal rule: ordinary space/tab padding still merges identity, while multiline injection text remains verbatim in source info and the generated description; the generated name uses accepted U+2028 separators. No language/content heuristic or narrower product rule was introduced.

### Commands and results: P1 fix round 2

All executions are **unit / mock_contract / replay on CPU**, with tiny synthetic inputs and labelled doubles. `P=<project>/.venv/Scripts/python.exe`; environment: `PYTHONNOUSERSITE=1`, `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`, `PYTHONPATH=<worktree>/src`. Every pytest/probe/mutant command goes through `P -B tools/agent-workflow/test_slot.py -- ...`. The owned scratch launcher expands to `P -B -X faulthandler -m pytest -q -o cache_dir=<worktree>/.tmp/T-010/fix2-cache-<name> -o faulthandler_timeout=120 --basetemp=<worktree>/.tmp/T-010/fix2-pytest-<name> <tests/selection>`, disables unrelated plugin autoload, keeps Hypothesis caches in owned scratch, starts its child with `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`, records/awaits its PID, bounds execution and propagates its real exit. Each retained log begins with its actual argv and selected source tree. Scratch baseline/mutant imports explicitly use that copy's `src` and `tests`.

## P1 fix round 3

### Step 0: approved by PM before implementation

**Dispatch:** fresh Codex implementer, assigned effort `high`, base `2df55cd`; R-007, R-014, R-015, R-017, R-018, R-026. This submission is the separate plan deliverable required by the Codex wrapper and brief footer. No runtime or test edit is included.

**Goal:** remove generic-client ownership of naming demand, precisely refuse malformed manifest artifact paths, and add detecting regressions for N2, N9, N8 and R9b while preserving lazy, merged, single-attempt, bounded backend proofs.

**Scope:** `src/prompt_enhancer/model_client.py`, `k2_naming.py`, `storage/store.py`; relevant K2/model/storage tests, including a small round-3 regression module if useful; `docs/stages/K2-observe.md` if its demand description needs updating; this task file. The other four audited model modules are read-only unless a directly necessary validation fix is demonstrated and recorded. No new deployment key, provider/model choice, GC/locking policy, later stage, shared PM record, Git write, network, model call, GPU, download or install.

**May assume:** the dispatch's PM decisions govern this round pending user review; use a keyword-only `require=()` argument and tiny labelled doubles without adding profile options. Update old tests whose implicit naming-demand assumption is explicitly superseded by caller-owned demand. Preserve existing defaults and backend/fallback proof ownership. **Must ask:** a conflict with a recorded decision, new product semantics/profile fields, expanded write scope or live work. No unresolved product question has been found.

**Unknowns and failure modes:**

- Demand must pass through `ModelClientRun.for_role`, `BoundModelClient.for_role`, capability ensuring/startup and actual multi-image calls. Losing it at any boundary can leave evidence absent; forwarding it to another backend can attach the wrong proof. A role without explicit demand must not spend multi-image requests, even when naming contains a sentinel or is absent.
- K2 already calls `validate_naming_reads` before source access (`k2_naming.py:299`), but the new comparison must remain after that check. Invalid/sentinel sample sizes need key-specific `ConfigurationError`; a generic module must never consult `knowledge.*`.
- N2 is masked by an exhausted allowance; use `max_requests=20`, repeated binds, and both negative/incomplete outcomes. N9 is masked by non-yielding doubles; yield within base and multi probes and gather binds. N8 needs an actual referenced draw whose checkpoint is removed, preserving the manifest reference and artifact.
- Manifest path validation must precede indexing/filtering and refuse malformed artifact entries even when they would otherwise be another kind. Errors must identify the snapshot and entry, rather than silently skip or leak `IndexError`.
- Reviewer probes a/b print observations rather than assert fixes. Probe c assumes automatic demand, which this round deliberately removes. Preserve and run originals before/after; report changed observations/obsolete assumptions honestly, and use an explicitly-demanding companion to verify the same guard behavior under the approved API.
- Whole-suite runtime was 830.52 seconds in round 2. Bound the once-only final run at 2400 seconds, keep children hidden and awaited, and stop/report if the same implementation/check step fails twice.

**Ranked approval point (1):** approve this plan and its evidence distinction: behavioral regressions fail on round 2; new guard-gap regressions pass on correct round 2 guards and kill N2/N9/N8/R9b. Proposed answer: approve and implement within this scope. The Codex wrapper requires a separate approval message before runtime/test edits.

**PM approval:** "Plan approved; implement it." Behavioral red/green versus correct-guard baseline passes is approved; retain original reviewer c observations and use an explicitly demanding companion. `require=()` is keyword-only and never forwarded to fallback. Generic model modules must read no `knowledge.*` key, with an assertion test. A 2400-second full-suite limit is approved. Ambiguity outside these decisions stops only the affected item, with a proposed answer under `Noticed, not done`.

### Class audit first (S31): reads outside the requesting role and its backend

Source-verified inventory of the current round-2 tree. `load_config` validates the merged profile with `enhancer_config.v0` and `_validate_model_bindings` (`config.py:1104-1109`), then refuses sentinels in command reads and validates concrete role schemas (`1134-1153`). `_sentinel` only rejects sentinels recursively (`609-617`); it does not validate arbitrary types. Direct low-level constructors assume a loaded configuration; the table does not claim they independently validate a raw dictionary. Ownership below distinguishes deliberate shared inputs from the defect being removed.

| Module/site | Outside-section key/read | Owner | Validation before read / disposition |
|---|---|---|---|
| `model_client.py:36-39` | `vlm.backends` catalogue; each backend's `image.max_decode_pixels` for Pillow's shared backstop | Run resource owner; each backend owns its image limit | Loader schema validates configured backend objects; constructor reads only object/image objects and exact integer limits. This includes unrelated backends, deliberately for the process backstop; the guard skips unresolved/non-integer values rather than treating them as active limits. |
| `model_client.py:40-42` | Every backend's `max_concurrency` | Run-level `BackendConcurrency`, limits owned per backend | Loader schema; constructor accepts only object entries with exact integer concurrency. Aggregation is intentional, not stage demand. |
| `model_client.py:43-44` | Every backend's `call_budget` | Run-level `SingleRunBudgetLedger`, grants owned per backend | Loader schema; constructor includes only exact integer grants. No implicit cloud grant is introduced. |
| `model_client.py:57-61,323-325,384-387` | Shared `vision` object and `vision.max_image_side` when role selects `from_vision` | Shared image preprocessing profile; role explicitly selects inheritance | Loader schema and command-read sentinel pass; `for_role` sentinel-checks the selected inherited leaf. K2 additionally validates all `vision` via `validate_naming_reads:974`. Image preparation validates positive integer/`none` size (`model_image.py:100-103`). Request hashing reads the inherited leaf too. |
| `model_client.py:62-63,326-327,384-387` | `vision.format` when role selects `from_vision` | Shared image preprocessing profile | Loader schema, encoder/media-type validation (`config.py:762-773`), command sentinel pass; `for_role` sentinel-checks the inherited format; `_call` rejects no-media-type output at `331-333`. Included in request hashing. |
| `model_client.py:340,384-387` | `vision.alpha_policy.mode` and conditional `.background` | Shared image preprocessing profile | Loader schema and command sentinel pass; K2 `checked('vision')`; `_check_settings` validates exact policy shape and background structure (`model_image.py:104-111`). Included in request hashing even with explicit role size/format. No shared `vision.quality` is read here: role image quality is used. |
| `model_client.py:64-70,269,291,406` | `vlm.default_backend` | Global model routing/fallback policy | `_validate_model_bindings:678-686` requires existing local backend/chat endpoint when resolved; loader command-read sentinel pass (`1146-1150`); `for_role:65` refuses unresolved default. |
| `model_client.py:66-70,263-264` on fallback | Default/fallback backend object, including provider/model/endpoint/image/probe/HTTP/budget fields used downstream | The selected fallback backend, distinct from the role's primary backend | Loader backend schema/bindings and command sentinel pass; `for_role:68` checks both selected backend objects. Fallback must retain its own evidence and budgets. Do not attach a primary role's production pair to it. |
| `model_client.py:143` | `vlm.roles` catalogue lookup for the requested role | Role registry / requesting role | Loaded profile schema; `for_role:53-54` resolves and sentinel-checks that role first. Low-level `role_name=None` callers can lack a role catalogue; `.get` intentionally yields no role/demand. No other role's configuration is read here. |
| `model_client.py:147` | `knowledge.naming.sample_size` | K2 naming stage | **Defect:** generic binding has no naming validation; sentinel comparison can raise raw `TypeError`. K2 validates it in `validate_naming_reads:946-961` before its own use. Remove this read entirely; K2 passes explicit capability demand. |
| `model_probe.py:23-203` | No configuration-tree lookup outside a role/backend; caller supplies probe cases, token/model identity, request allowance, timeout and reasoning observer | Probe caller/backend | `probe_capabilities:29-31` checks exact positive request count and finite positive timeout; pair helpers validate challenge structure. No `knowledge`, `vision`, role catalogue or global routing read. |
| `model_mapping.py:18-67` | None: provider, requested role sampling/token fields, reasoning fragment and capability evidence are arguments | Requested role and backend evidence | Loaded role/backend schema and binding checks are caller preconditions. Mapping performs no global configuration lookup. |
| `model_transport.py:28-70` | None outside its own supplied backend settings: provider, HTTP leaves and first base URL | Backend transport | Loaded backend schema/binding/timer checks (`config.py:1110-1122`); `endpoint_url:10-25` validates URL/route structure at use. Shared concurrency/ledger are injected resources, not another stage's keys. |
| `model_image.py:56-62,65-82` | `vision.max_image_side` passed as a shared settings dictionary | Shared image preprocessing profile / K1 caller | K1 `embedder_settings` calls `validate_embedder_reads` before resolving vision (`k1_embed.py:86-104`); `_vision_args` checks sentinels; `_check_settings` checks the numeric domain. No root config lookup. |
| Same image sites | `vision.format` | Shared image preprocessing profile | Same K1 preflight; `_vision_args` sentinel check; `_check_settings:98-99` verifies installed encoder. |
| Same image sites | `vision.quality` | Shared image preprocessing profile | Same K1 preflight validates its schema numeric domain; `_vision_args` sentinel check. `_check_settings` itself does not validate quality, so direct helper callers must supply validated settings. |
| Same image sites, `104-111,143-146` | `vision.alpha_policy.mode` and conditional `.background` (or explicit caller policy) | Shared image preprocessing profile / explicit image caller | Same K1 preflight; exact policy shape/color checked by `_check_settings` before bytes are decoded. |
| `model_image.py:14-22` | Aggregate decode limits supplied by run/K1; `Image.MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS` is a library global, not a profile key | Process decode backstop | Model-client aggregate uses exact integers; K1 validates its backend limit before invoking. No additional stage key is consulted. |

No `vision.transport` read exists in these five modules. Backend probe/image/HTTP settings and role output/reasoning/sampling/image settings are within the allowed owner sections and are excluded except where the run deliberately aggregates multiple backends or routes to fallback.

Identifiers G1-G8 refer to `.tmp/T-010/approach-note-gemini.md:49` through `:70`; C1-C15 refer to `.tmp/T-010/premortem-claude.md:7` through `:21`. Test names below are proposed, not executed evidence.

| --- | --- |
| G1, C1: direct/second-order injection | `test_observer_names_are_json_data` uses multilingual/generated names with quotes/newlines/closing delimiters and asserts exact system bytes; `test_planted_image_text_stays_stored_data` preserves a synthetic planted text payload verbatim in `text_in_image`, with no instruction interpolation. Real OCR obedience and later K3 prompt containment are not established by a double; carry the latter to P3. |
| G2: semantic rules / handwritten facets | `test_observation_parser_preserves_arbitrary_facet_and_value` property over Unicode; only structural/protocol validation, no word matching. Grouping uses numeric K1 evidence (Q2), never concept-name/description similarity. Retain all valid minority values; P3 grouping remains model-owned. |
| G3, C5: hidden defaults / sentinel bypass | `test_each_observe_leaf_refuses_before_activity`, `test_each_observer_leaf_refuses_before_activity`, plus non-default effects for every consumed leaf. Test both standalone and loaded configuration; refuse before client binding/source reads/checkpoints. Existing whole-command unresolved K3/K4/K5 settings still refuse `kb sync`. |
| G4, C4: persisted work / repeat calls | Naming replay remains in the supplied K2 regressions. P2 `test_observe_resume_has_no_extra_calls`, interruption after committed reply/before result assembly, empty-observations replay, and `test_corrupt_observation_artifact_is_not_reused`. No exactly-once claim for a crash after an external reply but before durable commit. |
| G5, C9: knowledge-base writes / weak Windows attributes | `test_observe_preserves_source_tree` compares names, sizes, mtimes and content fingerprints of a synthetic source tree before/after success and failure; guard resolved write targets and exercise links/overlap. No claim based on a Windows read-only directory attribute. Storage writes are allowed only under the synthetic configured store. |
| G6: validation bypass | `test_schema_invalid_observation_commits_no_rows` includes malformed JSON, missing/extra fields, invalid visibility/group, forged IDs, wrong positions and invalid regions. Other valid images finish. Validate both model-facing and code-assembled stored forms. |
| G7, C10: resize / repeated preparation / image failure | `test_observer_4000x3000_effective_side` inspects the encoded attachment and request hash with alternate role and inherited limits, aspect ratio and a preparation-call spy. `test_bad_image_fails_only_its_key` covers changed content, undecodable bytes, alpha refusal and decode cap; later valid images continue. |
| G8, C7, C8: majority vote / model-owned card labels | P3-only decisions remain deferred. P2 `test_observe_preserves_all_valid_facets` ensures raw observations are not voted away, no labels/counts/cards are authored, and unbounded finite quality is not converted into a probability. |
| C2: trusting model IDs/provenance | `test_observation_positions_are_bound_by_code` property rejects unknown/bool/duplicate-invalid labels and forged pipeline fields, maps known positions to the exact caller-owned concepts, and takes model provenance from the actual call record, including fallback. |
| C3: duplicate facet loses valid batch | `test_duplicate_facet_rejects_only_image` and parser property span same-facet/different-concept (valid), same-concept/same-facet including different groups (reject), and subsequent good images. Q1 covers collisions across independently valid contexts. |
| C6: free strings / false switches ignored | `test_observe_policy_refusals_before_activity` refuses unknown region/schema values and unsupported false switches instead of taking a silent else branch. Single-behavior fields are explicit audit exceptions. |
| C11: multi-image proof unavailable | Keep existing naming proof/refusal and fallback tests. P2 `test_single_image_observer_needs_no_multiple_proof`; four P3 regressions cover exact demands, repeated direct starts and backend-only plans without resetting allowances. |
| C12: K0 status confusion | `test_observe_skips_refused_and_unresolved_folders` ensures duplicate-name/protected/pending folders do not receive observation or accidental renaming; valid folders still run. Observation requires K1 completion, unlike accepted P1 null-status handling. |

Only approved group metadata, observation artifacts, checkpoints and returned report change. |

Corrections retained from Claude: current paths and COMMAND_READS are authoritative; K1 exists but grouping does not; no closed facet list, invented 0.20 threshold, region probe or Windows attribute-only write test. The P1 naming draw/override gaps in both old notes are already closed. Do not repeat those implementations.

### Ranked questions for PM approval (unsettled by stage docs / decisions)

1. **Storage identity across contexts:** the same content image can belong to folders declaring `{A}` and `{A, B}` (`contracts/README.md:17`, `:22`), and decision 7 requires separate calls/cache identities, but both may return facet A with the same physical `observations` PK (`storage/tables.py:101`). Which representation should preserve both without discarding a valid context? **Proposed answer:** approve one artifact-bound `context_key` column in the observations contract/table and PK, derived from the full image-call cache key. Store both contexts without overwriting or picking a winner; return artifacts and rows only. One committed image-call bundle/checkpoint covers its exact name set and all resulting per-concept rows, so replay does not need extra calls. This requires the corresponding storage fixture/contract tests; it is not a new profile option. If the PM instead requires a different table contract, settle it before implementation.
2. **Grouping algorithm and missing setting:** the stage names a grouping value but neither a metric nor clustering rule (`docs/stages/K2-observe.md:12`, `:14`); K1 has no grouping implementation. **Proposed answer:** add `observe.near_duplicate_cosine_distance: fit_from_gt`; use connected components of current K1 vectors whose cosine distance is at most the configured value, with stable content-derived group IDs and sorted `image_id` traversal. Compute groups consistently across the current K1 image set, then restrict group membership per folder for selection, so the single `images.near_duplicate_group` field is unambiguous. Validate finite/nonzero vectors and a single embedding space; avoid an all-pairs matrix by bounded scans. This is a proposed exact algorithm, not an experiment-proven threshold or a performance claim; quadratic worst-case CPU cost remains a limit to assess before accepting large-scale deployment. No numeric threshold is fitted here.
3. **Representative allocation when group count and quota differ:** sorted-image tie-breaking is settled, but neither the stage nor decision 7 says whether the quota caps distinct groups, is filled with duplicates when groups are few, or how to choose among excess groups. **Proposed answer:** group order is each group's least `image_id`; take one least-ID member from each group in that order, then fill any remaining positive quota from sorted remaining image IDs. Stop at `min(quota, eligible distinct image count)`; queue the rest. This yields the stated representative count while prioritizing group diversity and requires no random seed, largest-group heuristic or invented quality score. A zero quota is refused rather than silently indexing nothing. Approve a different allocation before code if intended.
4. **Model-facing schema split:** decision 3 prohibits model-authored IDs/provenance, while `image_observation.v0` requires them and the observer profile uses that schema. **Proposed answer:** preserve `image_observation.v0` as the stored document and add `observer_reply.v0` with `view`, finite `quality`, and observations labelled by zero-based `concept_position`, plus the existing facet/value/visibility/region fields. Update the observer's example output schema ID and prompt documentation; map positions and construct provenance/IDs in code, then validate the assembled stored document. No pipeline IDs are exposed to the model. This avoids changing the existing table enum/region schema pointers and rejects forged ID/origin fields through `additionalProperties:false`.

Set `PYTHONNOUSERSITE=1`, `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`, `PYTHONPATH=<project>/src`; scratch/cache/Hypothesis and the one fixed pytest basetemp stay under `.tmp/T-010-p2/`.
```text
P -B tools/agent-workflow/test_slot.py -- P -B -X faulthandler -m pytest -q tests/test_k2_*.py tests/test_model_client.py tests/test_model_probe.py tests/test_model_image.py tests/test_k1_*.py tests/test_storage*.py --basetemp=.tmp/T-010-p2/pytest

```

Expand test globs into explicit paths portably in the launcher; pytest does not expand a Windows glob itself. Run lint/format only if ruff is already present; P1 recorded it absent, and installation is forbidden. Bound the full suite above P1's recorded 857-second duration; do not repeat a failing step more than twice or repeat a passed full gate without a new cause. Delete owned basetemp/mutant/recall/launcher trees after verification, retain small cited logs, and await/stop only recorded owned children. No model-quality, speed, GPU, cloud or Linux result is inferred from these CPU tests.

- **Executed evidence:** project-venv `python -B -I tools/agent-workflow/recall.py --help` printed usage; `build --db .tmp/T-010-p2/recall.sqlite` returned `indexed=92 unchanged=0 removed=0 refused=0 chunks=610 skipped_chunks=0`. Searches `observation` and `representatives` used that index; the former returned `tasks/STATUS.md:74`, the latter no hits. Settled source documents were read directly. `git diff --check -- tasks/active/T-010-k2-k3.md` exited 0 with no output; scoped `git diff --numstat -- src/ contracts/ prompts/ configs/ docs/ tests/` exited 0 with no output. The owned recall database and its empty task scratch directory were removed; `Test-Path` returned `False` for each.
- **Tools/limitations:** `rg` is absent; dedicated Read/Grep tools are not exposed, and Node REPL was rejected by the role allowlist, so bounded PowerShell reads/searches were used. A malformed array-based read command and an attempted `GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS` environment assignment were rejected by hooks; corrected read-only calls succeeded. An unscoped read-only Git status reported a pre-existing missing `.secrets/README.md`; it was not opened, investigated or modified. Subsequent diff checks are explicitly scoped to the task file.
- **not_run:** runtime implementation, tests, mutants, lint/format, live/model/GPU/network work and CI. This is the required plan-only message; no implementation pass or completed Configuration audit is claimed. There is no changed operational value to audit yet; the impact inventory defines the future audit.
- **Current status:** `PLANNING`, awaiting explicit PM approval and Q1-Q4 answers. Next: approve/amend the four proposed choices and send the implementation message. Do not repeat accepted P1 work, fit sentinels, narrow `kb sync` reads, author semantic rules or spend live calls. No process/service was started for the pipeline.
- **Noticed, not done:** the missing P3 numeric low-diversity setting remains owned by P3; the accepted once-per-run P1 storage/recovery cost is unchanged. The stage's K1 grouping wording is clarified by the actual source above, not by assuming groups exist.

### PM approval and implementation progress

PM approved implementation and Q1-Q4 in the follow-up message, as PM decisions pending user review: add artifact-bound observation `context_key`; cosine-distance connected components and a new sentinel setting; group-first deterministic quota filling; `observer_reply.v0` split; all four P3 dispositions. Every example explicitly declares the new distance sentinel. The approved Configuration impact above is the pre-edit gate; no fitting/live work or additional deployment setting is authorized.

1. Completed: Step 0 and the four decisions approved; primary code/contracts/tests re-read by section.
   In progress: four P3 fixes, observation contracts/configuration preflight and prompt; no later-stage implementation.

2. Completed: four P3 fixes, context-aware stored/model contracts, explicit example sentinels, strict observer preflight, prompt, deterministic grouping/selection and resumable observation/queue implementation.
   In progress: focused verification. Initial new-suite result `4 failed, 242 passed in 74.47s`; fixed one real nonfinite-reply isolation gap and three fixture/API errors. Next result `1 failed, 264 passed in 97.34s`; all prior failures closed, one newly added fallback fixture omitted its already-started probe allowance, now corrected. No model/network/GPU work.

**Additional conservative PM decision pending user review:** reuse the configured observer backend's `max_concurrency` for bounded batches, with transport-owned fallback limits. Cancel and await sibling tasks on a fatal batch error before writer/run teardown. No new worker/deadline key or performance claim. This applies the existing concurrency/lifecycle rules without changing observation meaning.

3. Completed: broad focused gate `2600 passed, 12 skipped, 4 warnings in 761.26s`; final affected gate `94 passed in 50.56s`. An added zero-distance regression first showed `1 failed, 1 passed in 4.05s`; identical normalized vectors now have exact zero distance without an epsilon. Fatal-batch sibling cancellation passed.
   In progress: all eight changed-line scratch mutants killed (zero survivors), including Q5; the one required whole pipeline run is executing. No runtime edit is pending. Lint/format are not_run because ruff is absent; no install is permitted.

4. Completed: permitted validation and cleanup; sole once-only full-run overlay failure fixed, affected loader/config retest `480 passed`, ninth guard mutant killed; all nine mutants killed with zero survivors. Final hashes match 25 non-report changed files, AST parses 15 Python files, whitespace check passes, owned scratch/helpers removed.

### Configuration audit (P2)

Audit inventory for the completed runtime diff, corrected by fix round 1. Test execution status is reported in the P2 command tables; this table is not a claim that a pending command passed. Each singleton exception is explicit; a fabricated second supported value would contradict the approved contract. Backend rows apply separately to the selected observer backend and the configured default when distinct. Base/dev/deploy means the effective example configurations: the deployment overlay inherits fitted observe values from its full base profile and cannot declare or override them. Existing shared settings retain their owning T-005/T-006/T-009 tests; P2 adds direct observer-bound refusal/override coverage where it reads them.

### Completion report: P2

**Work completed (R-007, R-010, R-014, R-015, R-017, R-018, R-026):** implemented `k2_observe.observe_images` over accepted P1 naming rows and current K1 vectors, with deterministic global groups/per-folder representatives, exactly one prepared observer image per call, positioned names-only data, per-image validation/failure isolation, context-aware rows/bundle checkpoints, durable replay and tier-3 queues. The four inherited P3 notes are closed by exact public `require` validation, a repeated-direct-start test killing Q5, early draw-suffix refusal, and the preserved backend-only-plan contract. The stage never publishes or runs later stages. All new numerical test values are labelled; example thresholds remain sentinels.

**Changed files (26 including this task):** runtime `src/prompt_enhancer/{k2_observe.py,config.py,model_client.py,storage/identity.py,storage/tables.py}`; contracts `contracts/{README.md,schemas/enhancer_config.v0.schema.json,schemas/image_observation.v0.schema.json,schemas/observer_reply.v0.schema.json}`; profiles `configs/{enhancer.example.yaml,enhancer.dev.example.yaml,overlays/deploy-vllm.example.yaml}`; prompts/docs `prompts/{observer_v0.txt,README.md}`, `docs/stages/K2-observe.md`; tests `tests/{conftest.py,storage_fixtures.py,test_k0_config.py,k2_observe_fixtures.py,test_k2_observe.py,test_k2_observe_config.py,test_k2_observe_edges.py,test_k2_observe_properties.py,test_k2_observe_shared_config.py,test_k2_p3_notes.py}`; this task file. The K0 test fixture now names the actual facet contract, and shared synthetic fixtures explicitly resolve the new grouping threshold/observer source; no old behavior is special-cased.

**Important changes/reasons:** approved `context_key` preserves overlapping concept-set contexts instead of dropping one; the model/stored schema split keeps pipeline IDs/provenance code-owned. Exact normalized-vector equality preserves mathematical zero distance without an unmeasured epsilon. Configured backend concurrency bounds prepared image/request work, and fatal errors cancel/await siblings before writer/run teardown. Validated regions remain unused. Single-link chaining and quadratic worst-case CPU are documented fit-time limitations, not performance claims. Old draft observation tables require rebuilding; no context is guessed for them.

#### Actual commands and results: P2

All results are **unit / mock_contract / replay / property, bounded CPU**, using synthetic fixtures and labelled doubles; never live quality/performance evidence. `P` is the shared project venv interpreter, `<project>/.venv/Scripts/python.exe`; commands start in this worktree. `check.py` sets `PYTHONNOUSERSITE=1`, `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`, `PYTHONPATH=<worktree>/src`, scratch-local pytest/Hypothesis caches, plugin autoload off and faulthandler. The one fixed basetemp is `.tmp/T-010-p2/pytest`.

The exact launcher stack is `P -B .tmp/T-010-p2/check.py --name NAME --seconds LIMIT <test paths>` -> hidden `P -B .tmp/T-010-p2/slot.py P -B -X faulthandler .tmp/T-010-p2/payload.py NAME -q <expanded paths> --basetemp=.tmp/T-010-p2/pytest`. `slot.py` invokes `tools/agent-workflow/test_slot.py` with that child command; `payload.py` calls `pytest.main` and records its real status/PID/arguments. This keeps actual interpreter output inside the owned log despite the Windows venv redirector. Every pytest/mutant/probe is limiter-controlled. All Windows check children use `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`; final mutants/full-suite also enforce that flag for children created inside tests. The final harness uses `faulthandler_timeout=60` and `faulthandler_exit_on_timeout=true`, verified against installed `_pytest/faulthandler.py:23-29`, `:91-96`; the whole-suite outer bound is 2400 seconds. No xdist workers are enabled.
